## Limits And Additional Notes

- Historical private worker03 review is preserved as bounded findings and assessment provenance; this bundle does not invent terminal native previous_occurrences or close GitHub issues.
- J887 exact receiving applicability and MediaDive1886-to-JCM187 bridge remain unresolved; failed captures remain failed.
- Unknown product formulations, exact forms, solvent quantities, gas content, percent bases and lot/SKU identity remain explicitly bounded in decisions and payloads.
- EBIOS cross-worker proposal selection remains open under issue1221, filed before the bounded proposal correction.
- Only proposed artifacts are changed; no maintained YAML, SSSOM, canonical rebuild, PR, merge or external paid session.

  path: reports/all_unmatched_20261107/worker-06/research-c/after/N-Z-Amine_A.yaml
  label: Bounded raw-source alias addition with existing identity/status and prior
    decisions preserved; The primary JCM437 table spells N-Z Amine,type A(Sheffield),
    resolving the doubled dash in the frozen label while retaining the type-A qualifier.
    The supplier description confirms N-Z-Amine A is an enzymatic casein digest; this
    is not pure casein, TypeB or N-Z-Case by lexical similarity. Combined with independently
    checked B71 raw lowercase variant from six JCM recipes; retains UNMAPPED and does
    not assert supplier catalogue equivalence. Successor-v3 adds a metadata-only backlink
    for the seven explicitly Sheffield/typeA receiving contexts of source1608; four
    unspecified contexts remain excluded. The exact TypeA owner payload is unchanged.

- target_id: C-P-JCM151
  path: reports/all_unmatched_20261107/worker-06/successor-v3/research-a/payloads/Trace_Minerals_See_Medium_No_151.yaml
  label: Source-bound stock decomposition, numbered aliases and exact formulation
    registry promotion. The nested chain806→262→265→151 identifies the JCM151 trace-mineral
    preparation, not the complete Pyrococcus medium. Its NTA/sulfate-salt recipe supports
    correcting NAMED_MEDIUM to STOCK_SOLUTION and transcribing the minerals, water
    and KOH adjustment. Variable MnSO4·xH2O remains source text. The complete numbered
    formulation now uses its own source-defined kgmicrobe.ingredient registry identity;
    prior UNMAPPED retention was a conservative status choice, not an unresolved identity
    boundary. Successor-v3 corrects only source-local alias admission and qualifying
    labels under W06-IR-002.

- target_id: C-P-JCM284
  path: reports/all_unmatched_20261107/worker-06/successor-v3/research-a/payloads/Trace_Vitamins_Solution_See_Medium_No_284.yaml
  label: Source-bound stock decomposition, numbered aliases and exact formulation
    registry promotion. JCM1375 explicitly references the trace-vitamin solution of284.
    The284 table has different vitamin amounts from197 and explicitly DL-calcium pantothenate;
    the asterisk marks preparation instructions and must not merge vitamin and mineral
    stocks. The complete numbered formulation now uses its own source-defined kgmicrobe.ingredient
    registry identity; prior UNMAPPED retention was a conservative status choice,
    not an unresolved identity boundary. Successor-v3 corrects only source-local alias
    admission and qualifying labels under W06-IR-002.

  - repository: CultureBotAI/MediaIngredientMech

- target_id: C-P-JCM197
  path: reports/all_unmatched_20261107/worker-06/successor-v3/research-a/payloads/JCM_197_Trace_Vitamins.yaml
  label: 'JCM197 defines ten vitamins and water with explicit amounts. This is a recipe-defined
    stock, not a generic vitamin chemical class. Existing Wolfe/ATCC-labelled stocks
    are not automatically identical: their phosphate or pantothenate-form differences
    preclude a lexical merge. A source-specific local identity is proposed. NBRC837
    ten-vitamin formulation independently compared and linked only in that source
    occurrence. Successor-v3 corrects only source-local alias admission and qualifying
    labels under W06-IR-002.'
  kind: source
  ownership_note: Isolated uninstalled ingredient proposal. Proposed owner paths are
    intake metadata; only the coordinator may apply maintained changes after separate
    acceptance.
- target_id: C-P-JCM899
  path: reports/all_unmatched_20261107/worker-06/successor-v3/research-a/payloads/JCM_899_Trace_Element_Solution.yaml
  label: The numbered reference identifies the acidified trace-element formulation
    in JCM899, not a chemical named Trace Element. Its recipe and receiving-media
    reference are available; the formulation must remain distinct from JCM187,439
    and1079. Dissolve ammonium iron sulfate in25%HCl before diluting with water; add
    remaining salts in source order. Water995ml and acid5ml are source batch inputs;
    no molarity or percent basis beyond the source is inferred. Successor-v3 corrects
    only source-local alias admission and qualifying labels under W06-IR-002.
  kind: source
  ownership_note: Isolated uninstalled ingredient proposal. Proposed owner paths are
    intake metadata; only the coordinator may apply maintained changes after separate
    acceptance.
- target_id: C-P-JCM1079
  path: reports/all_unmatched_20261107/worker-06/successor-v3/research-a/payloads/JCM_1079_Trace_Element_Solution.yaml
  label: JCM1079 defines an EDTA-containing acidic trace-element stock with FeSO4
    heptahydrate and other salts, adjusted to pH3.6. The explicit1079 reference prevents
    identity with187,439 or899 despite similar names. Source requires pH3.6 but does
    not name a titrant. UNKNOWN completeness preserves that unresolved preparation
    constituent; do not invent HCl/NaOH or a salt form of EDTA. Successor-v3 corrects
    only source-local alias admission and qualifying labels under W06-IR-002.
  kind: source
  ownership_note: Isolated uninstalled ingredient proposal. Proposed owner paths are
    intake metadata; only the coordinator may apply maintained changes after separate
    acceptance.
- target_id: C-P-JCM284-MINERALS
  path: reports/all_unmatched_20261107/worker-06/successor-v3/research-a/payloads/JCM_284_Trace_Element_Solution.yaml
  label: This is the trace-element solution of284, not that same medium’s trace-vitamin
    solution. JCM284 explicitly supplies separate mineral and vitamin tables and a
    third Se/W stock. NTA is first dissolved in200ml water with KOH to pH6.5, then
    salts added and volume brought to1.0L; final pH7.0 with KOH. Water1L denotes total
    source stock volume, not1L plus200ml. Distinct from284vitamin and Se/W solutions.
    Successor-v3 corrects only source-local alias admission and qualifying labels
    under W06-IR-002.
  kind: source
  ownership_note: Isolated uninstalled ingredient proposal. Proposed owner paths are
    intake metadata; only the coordinator may apply maintained changes after separate
    acceptance.
- target_id: C-P-JCM1375-1435-825-GLUCOSE10
  path: reports/all_unmatched_20261107/worker-06/successor-v3/research-c/payloads/jcm_1375_1435_825_glucose_10_percent_stock.yaml
  label: 'Each source specifies separately autoclaved sterile anaerobic 10% glucose
    stock: JCM1375 adds 100 ml, JCM1435 adds 5 ml, and JCM825 adds 50 ml to its receiving
    batch. The percent basis, glucose chirality/anomer/starting hydrate and stock
    final volume are unstated. PERCENT deliberately does not assert w/v. The source
    does not identify the stock solvent. Water is omitted from components; UNKNOWN
    closure remains. Do not merge with filter-sterilized ETSA 10% stock or 20% stocks
    by label alone. Successor v3 omits untranscribed Water; the stated solute concentration
    and source preparation remain unchanged.'
  kind: source
  ownership_note: Isolated uninstalled ingredient proposal. Proposed owner paths are
    intake metadata; only the coordinator may apply maintained changes after separate
    acceptance.
- target_id: C-P-TOGO2790-GLUCOSE20
  path: reports/all_unmatched_20261107/worker-06/successor-v3/research-c/payloads/togo_2790_glucose_20_percent_stock.yaml
  label: The M9 source narrative supplies 2.5 ml of 20% glucose per 100 ml receiving
    medium. It does not state the percent basis, glucose form, stock preparation volume
    or sterilization. The stock solvent is not identified. Water is omitted from components;
    UNKNOWN closure and PERCENT preserve these limits. This is not automatically equivalent

- evidence_id: A-E-CAPTURE-084
  kind: primary_source
  reference: https://www.dsmz.de/microorganisms/medium/pdf/DSMZ_Medium1429.pdf

    source label and context named in each case applicability
  accessed_at: '2026-11-08T05:06:54.308106Z'
  support: context_only
  summary: 'case35: DSMZ1429 names Merck108339 Sabouraud2%glucose broth and adds30g/L;
    the supplier confirms a dehydrated broth composed of two peptone fractions plus20g/L
    D(+)glucose after reconstitution. The source also adds agar separately, so the
    broth product itself is not agar medium or pureglucose. Capture and actual historical
